[\[231b.1\]]bar langs te dam tshig la rang gi yi dam lha'i rnal 'byor dang ldan pa'i slob dpon gyis nye bar spyad pa lnga dang gtor ma dang mchod pa la sogs pa byas nas/_de nas gro ga'am dar bzang po la rtsibs brgyad pa'i 'khor lo bri bar bya'o/_/lte ba la maM gi yi ge bri'o/_/maM yig gi dbus

[\[231b.3\]]s+ya rak+ShaM ku ru swA hA/_lho'i rtsibs la/_oM mar+k+ka ma si a s+ya b+hU b+hA gA s+ya rak+ShaM ku ru swA hA/_nub kyi rtsibs la/_oM te dzo ma si a s+ya b+hU b+hA gA s+ya rak+ShaM ku ru swA hA/_byang gi rtsibs la/_oM ne ra ma si a s+ya b+hU b+hA gA s+ya rak+ShaM ku ru swA hA/_dbang ldan gyi rtsibs la/_oM tsi bA ra ma si a s+ya b+hU b+hA 
[\[231b.4\]]gA s+ya rak+ShaM ku ru swA hA/_rlung gi rtsibs la/_oM b+ha na ma si a s+ya b+hU b+hA gA s+ya rak+ShaM ku ru swA hA/_bden bral gyi rtsibs la/_oM gul+ma ma si a s+ya b+hU b+hA gA s+ya rak+ShaM ku ru swA hA/_me'i rtsibs la/_oM u da ya ma si a s+ya b+hU b+hA gA s+ya rak+ShaM ku ru shAn+tiM ku ru puSh+TiM ku ru ba shaM ku ru swA hA/_phyi'i
